### Assistant - 2026-04-25 04:17:43 EDT

````
是，直接上这个版本超参数确实偏多：

```text
temporal_weight
beta
m_min
m_scale
hidden_dim
feature choice
```

这会把实验变成另一个大 sweep，不划算。第一版应该压到**最多 2 个新超参数**，甚至 1 个。

我建议这样简化。

**方案 A：固定范围，只扫一个权重**
把 `m_t` 限死在一个合理范围，不扫 `m_min/m_scale/beta`：

```text
m_t = m_min + m_scale * sigmoid(g(sg(z_t), a_t))
m_min = 0.05
m_scale = 0.95
beta = 0.01 固定
hidden_dim = 128 固定
```

只扫：

```text
temporal_weight = 0.0003, 0.001, 0.003
```

这已经比 fixed hinge 更合理，而且实验量可控。

**方案 B：去掉 beta，用均值归一化**
让 `m_t` 的平均值固定，直接消除“全部变大”的自由度：

```python
m = sigmoid(g(...))              # (B,T-1), in (0,1)
m = m / m.detach().mean().clamp_min(1e-6)
m_t = base_margin * m
m_t = m_t.clamp(max=max_margin)
```

这样 `m_t` 只能在 transition 之间重新分配 margin，不能整体放大。loss 变成：

```text
max(0, d - m_t)^2
```

新超参数只剩：

```text
base_margin
temporal_weight
```

如果 `base_margin` 固定成前面相对较稳的 `0.5` 或 `1.0`，那只剩一个：

```text
temporal_weight
```

这个我更推荐。它的含义很清楚：

```text
固定平均允许步长，让网络学习哪些 transition 应该用更多 margin，哪些应该用更少 margin。
```

**方案 C：不用网络，先用 action rank**
如果只是快速验证，可以完全不要 learnable MLP：

```text
m_t = base_margin * normalized_rank(||a_t||)
```

但你前面说 action norm 跨任务不好统一；单任务内可以试，但它不如 learnable margin 有表达力。

**我建议第一版**
用 **mean-normalized dynamic margin**：

```python
raw = margin_net(torch.cat([z_t.detach(), a_t], dim=-1)).squeeze(-1)
score = torch.sigmoid(raw)

score = score / score.detach().mean().clamp_min(1e-6)
m_t = (base_margin * score).clamp(min=m_min, max=m_max)

loss = torch.clamp_min(dist - m_t, 0.0).square().mean()
```

固定：

```text
base_margin = 0.5
m_min = 0.05
m_max = 1.0
hidden_dim = 128
features = [sg(z_t), a_t]
```

只扫：

```text
temporal_weight = 0.0003, 0.001, 0.003
```

这样没有 `beta`，也不需要扫 margin scale。网络只能学习**margin 分配**，不能通过整体放大 margin 逃避约束。

这版实验目标也很清楚：  
**在平均连续性强度不变的前提下，学习哪些 transition 应该更连续、哪些应该允许跳变。**
````

### User - 2026-04-25 04:25:36 EDT

hidden_dim是只embed_dim吗，这样超参数也不少，只不过我们固定了其他几个而已，充分论证分析下咋样最合适

### Assistant - 2026-04-25 04:26:13 EDT

````
对，你这个质疑是对的：如果只是“固定一堆超参数”，本质上还是把 sweep 隐藏起来了。最合适的设计应该满足：

```text
1. 比 fixed hinge 多一点适应性
2. 不引入新的大超参数空间
3. 机制清楚，容易解释
4. 不依赖 state/proprio
5. 不破坏现有 SWM baseline
```

我建议第一版不要用 hidden MLP。`hidden_dim` 不是 `embed_dim`，它是 margin 网络内部宽度；但我们完全可以不用它。

**最合适的第一版：linear dynamic margin head**
不要 MLP，只用一个线性 head：

```text
score_t = sigmoid(w^T [sg(z_t), sg(a_emb_t)] + b)
```

然后做均值归一化：

```text
score_t = score_t / mean(score_t).detach()
m_t = base_margin * score_t
m_t = clamp(m_t, m_min, m_max)
loss = mean(max(0, d(z_t,z_{t+1}) - m_t)^2)
```

这样新增的可学习参数只有一个线性层：

```text
Linear(2 * embed_dim, 1)
```

如果用 raw action，则是：

```text
Linear(embed_dim + action_dim, 1)
```

但我更倾向用已有的 `act_emb_t`，因为它已经通过 `action_encoder` 进入动力学预测路径，语义上更对齐。

**为什么 linear 比 MLP 更合适**
MLP 表达力更强，但它会引入很多解释成本：

```text
hidden_dim
activation
depth
是否过拟合
是否学到数据集 idiosyncrasy
```

而 linear head 的假设更干净：

```text
在当前 latent/action embedding 空间里，学习一个方向，判断哪些 transition 允许更大 latent step。
```

这已经足够验证核心假设：

```text
固定平均 margin 的情况下，模型能否把 margin 分配给更需要跳变的 transition？
```

如果 linear 都没效果，直接上 MLP 大概率只是调参。  
如果 linear 有效果，再考虑 MLP 才有意义。

**超参数怎么压到最少**
用 mean-normalization 后，可以去掉 `beta`：

```text
mean(m_t) 固定为 base_margin
```

所以网络不能整体放大 margin 逃避约束，只能重新分配：

```text
有些 transition m_t 大
有些 transition m_t 小
平均强度不变
```

第一版真正需要扫的只剩一个：

```text
temporal_weight
```

其他全部固定为机制常数，不作为实验维度：

```text
base_margin = 0.5
m_min = 0.05
m_max = 1.0
head = Linear only
feature = [sg(z_t), sg(act_emb_t)]
```

这里 `m_min/m_max` 不是调参重点，只是数值安全边界。SWM 用 cosine distance：

```text
d = 1 - cos(z_t, z_{t+1}) ∈ [0, 2]
```

所以 `0.05` 和 `1.0` 是合理保护范围。

**我认为最稳的实现形式**
```python
z_feat = z_t.detach()
a_feat = act_emb_t.detach()

score = torch.sigmoid(margin_head(torch.cat([z_feat, a_feat], dim=-1))).squeeze(-1)
score = score / score.detach().mean().clamp_min(1e-6)

m_t = base_margin * score
m_t = m_t.clamp(min=0.05, max=1.0)

dist = 1.0 - (z_t * z_tp1).sum(dim=-1)
loss = torch.clamp_min(dist - m_t, 0.0).square().mean()
```

注意我建议第一版把 `z_t` 和 `act_emb_t` 都 `detach()`。这样 margin head 只是读特征，不反向改 encoder/action_encoder，避免它为了降低 regularizer 去扭曲主表示。主 encoder 仍然通过 hinge loss 的 `dist` 收到梯度。

**为什么不用 beta 版**
`beta * mean(m_t)` 理论上更灵活，但多一个敏感超参数。你现在的实验问题已经是超参数太多，所以第一版最好不要用 beta。均值归一化更干净：

```text
beta 版：学习 margin 大小 + margin 分配
mean-normalized 版：只学习 margin 分配
```

我们现在只想验证“自适应分配”有没有用，所以第二个更合适。

**实验设计**
不要大 sweep。四个任务各跑：

```text
baseline: no hinge SWM
fixed hinge: 已有结果
linear dynamic margin: temporal_weight=0.001
```

如果算力允许，再加：

```text
temporal_weight=0.0003
temporal_weight=0.003
```

但第一判断只需要 `0.001`。

必须记录这些诊断：

```text
m_t mean/std/p10/p50/p90
hinge active ratio
corr(m_t, dist)
dist p50/p90
eval score
```

成功信号不是单纯 `m_t` 变大，而是：

```text
PushT/Reacher: m_t 分布更宽，避免崩
TwoRoom: m_t 更集中，保留连续性收益
active ratio 不接近 0 或 1
eval 不低于 fixed hinge，最好接近或超过 no-hinge baseline
```

**最终建议**
第一版不要做“动态 margin MLP”。做：

```text
mean-normalized linear dynamic margin head
```

它只新增一个线性层，几乎没有结构超参数；固定平均 margin 后也不需要 `beta`。这样实验结论最干净：

```text
在不改变整体 continuity 强度的前提下，学习 transition-wise margin 分配是否比 fixed margin 更适合四个任务？
```

这比直接上复杂网络更适合当前阶段。
````

from utils import get_column_normalizer, get_img_preprocessor, ModelObjectCallBack


def resolve_norm_fn(norm_name: str):
    norm_name = norm_name.lower()
    if norm_name in {"none", "identity"}:
        return None
    if norm_name in {"ln", "layernorm"}:
        return nn.LayerNorm
    if norm_name in {"bn", "batchnorm", "batchnorm1d"}:
        return nn.BatchNorm1d
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported projection_head.norm_fn: {norm_name}")


def build_projection_head(input_dim: int, output_dim: int, cfg) -> nn.Module:
    head_type = cfg.type.lower()
    norm_name = cfg.get("norm_fn", "none")

    # Backward-compatible aliases for older configs.
    if head_type == "bn":
        head_type = "linear"
        norm_name = "batchnorm1d"
    elif head_type == "ln":
        head_type = "linear"
        norm_name = "layernorm"

    norm_fn = resolve_norm_fn(norm_name)

    if head_type == "linear":
        layers = [nn.Linear(input_dim, output_dim)]
        if norm_fn is not None:
            layers.append(norm_fn(output_dim))
        return nn.Sequential(*layers) if len(layers) > 1 else layers[0]

    if head_type == "mlp":
        return MLP(
            input_dim=input_dim,
            hidden_dim=cfg.get("hidden_dim", 2048),
            output_dim=output_dim,
            norm_fn=norm_fn,
        )

    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported projection_head.type: {head_type}")


def get_loss_space_tensors(output, *, pred_raw, pred_norm, n_preds: int, space: str):
    pred_len = pred_raw.size(1)
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return pred_raw, output["emb_raw"][:, n_preds : n_preds + pred_len]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return pred_norm, output["emb"][:, n_preds : n_preds + pred_len]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss space: {space}")


def get_regularizer_tensor(output, *, space: str):
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return output["emb_raw"]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return output["emb"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.space: {space}")


def get_context_tensor(output, *, space: str):
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return output["emb_raw"]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return output["emb"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.pred.context_space: {space}")


def get_embedding_tensor(output, *, space: str):
    space = space.lower()
    if space == "raw":
        return output["emb_raw"]
    if space in {"normalized", "sphere"}:
        return output["emb"]
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported embedding space: {space}")


def compute_embedding_loss(
    pred: torch.Tensor,
    target: torch.Tensor,
    *,
    loss_type: str,
) -> torch.Tensor:
    loss_type = loss_type.lower()
    if loss_type == "cosine":
        return (1.0 - F.cosine_similarity(pred, target, dim=-1)).mean()
    if loss_type == "mse":
        return F.mse_loss(pred, target)
    raise ValueError(f"Unsupported prediction loss type: {loss_type}")


def compute_pred_loss(pred: torch.Tensor, target: torch.Tensor, cfg) -> torch.Tensor:
    pred_type = cfg.loss.pred.get("type", "cosine")
    return compute_embedding_loss(pred, target, loss_type=pred_type)


def compute_multistep_rollout_loss(output, *, model, cfg):
    rollout_cfg = cfg.loss.get("rollout", {})
    rollout_steps = int(rollout_cfg.get("steps", 1))
    if rollout_cfg.get("weight", 0.0) <= 0.0 or rollout_steps <= 1:
        return None

    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
    available_future = output["emb"].size(1) - ctx_len
    rollout_steps = min(rollout_steps, available_future)
    if rollout_steps <= 1:
        return None

    context_space = rollout_cfg.get("context_space", cfg.loss.pred.get("context_space", cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized")))
    target_space = rollout_cfg.get("space", cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized"))
    loss_type = rollout_cfg.get("type", cfg.loss.pred.get("type", "cosine"))

    full_act_emb = output["act_emb"][:, : ctx_len + rollout_steps]
    rollout_raw = output["emb_raw"][:, :ctx_len].clone()
    rollout_norm = output["emb"][:, :ctx_len].clone()
    target = get_embedding_tensor(output, space=target_space)[:, ctx_len : ctx_len + rollout_steps]

    pred_steps = []
    for step in range(rollout_steps):
        action_end = ctx_len + step
        window = min(ctx_len, action_end)
        rollout_ctx = get_embedding_tensor(
            {"emb_raw": rollout_raw, "emb": rollout_norm},
            space=context_space,
        )[:, -window:]
        act_trunc = full_act_emb[:, action_end - window : action_end]

        pred_raw = model.predict_raw(rollout_ctx, act_trunc)[:, -1:]
        pred_norm = model.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)
        pred_steps.append(
            get_embedding_tensor({"emb_raw": pred_raw, "emb": pred_norm}, space=target_space)
        )

        rollout_raw = torch.cat([rollout_raw, pred_raw], dim=1)
        rollout_norm = torch.cat([rollout_norm, pred_norm], dim=1)

    if len(pred_steps) <= 1:
        return None

    # Keep the first rollout step only as autoregressive context so this
    # auxiliary term supervises strictly beyond the one-step pred_loss.
    pred = torch.cat(pred_steps[1:], dim=1)
    target = target[:, 1:]
    return compute_embedding_loss(pred, target, loss_type=loss_type)

def swm_forward(self, batch, stage, cfg):
    """Encode observations, predict next states, compute spherical losses.

    Losses:
      pred_loss   — configurable prediction loss in raw or normalized space
      reg_loss    — configurable anti-collapse loss in raw or normalized space
      temporal_hinge_loss — optional hinge loss on consecutive latent pairs
      loss        — pred_loss + λ_reg * reg_loss + λ_hinge * temporal_hinge_loss

    Note:
      temporal_straightness is logged as a monitoring metric using Euclidean
      displacement vectors between consecutive unit-norm latents. On the sphere
      this is only a chord-space approximation, so it may not reflect true
      geodesic straightening accurately.
    """
    ctx_len = cfg.wm.history_size
    n_preds = cfg.wm.num_preds
    reg_type = cfg.loss.regularizer.type
    reg_lambd = cfg.loss.regularizer.weight
    hinge_cfg = cfg.loss.temporal_hinge
    reg_space = cfg.loss.regularizer.get("space", "normalized")
    pred_space = cfg.loss.pred.get("space", "normalized")
    context_space = cfg.loss.pred.get("context_space", pred_space)

    # Replace NaN values with 0 (occurs at sequence boundaries)
    batch["action"] = torch.nan_to_num(batch["action"], 0.0)

    output = self.model.encode(batch)

    emb = output["emb"]  # (B, T, D), unit vectors
    act_emb = output["act_emb"]

    # Make training-time autoregressive context explicit so raw-consistent
    # experiments can feed predictor history from the same space used for the
    # prediction target and planning rollout.
    ctx_emb = get_context_tensor(output, space=context_space)[:, :ctx_len]
    ctx_act = act_emb[:, :ctx_len]

    pred_raw = self.model.predict_raw(ctx_emb, ctx_act)
    pred_emb = self.model.normalize_embeddings(pred_raw)

    pred_source, tgt_source = get_loss_space_tensors(
        output,
        pred_raw=pred_raw,
        pred_norm=pred_emb,
        n_preds=n_preds,
        space=pred_space,
    )
    output["pred_loss"] = compute_pred_loss(pred_source, tgt_source, cfg)
    output["rollout_loss"] = compute_multistep_rollout_loss(
        output,
        model=self.model,
        cfg=cfg,
    )

    reg_emb = get_regularizer_tensor(output, space=reg_space)
    reg_scope = cfg.loss.regularizer.get("scope", "full").lower()
    if reg_scope == "pred_window":
        reg_emb = reg_emb[:, : ctx_len + n_preds]
    elif reg_scope != "full":
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.scope: {reg_scope}")
    if reg_type == "spread":
        output["spread_loss"] = spread_loss(reg_emb, cfg.loss.spread.margin)
        output["reg_loss"] = output["spread_loss"]
    elif reg_type == "uniformity":
        output["uniformity_loss"] = uniformity_loss(
            reg_emb,
            cfg.loss.uniformity.t,
            mode=cfg.loss.uniformity.get("mode", "all_pairs"),
            temporal_exclusion=cfg.loss.uniformity.get("temporal_exclusion", 0),
        )
        output["reg_loss"] = output["uniformity_loss"]
    elif reg_type == "infonce":
        tgt_emb = output["emb"][:, n_preds:]
        output["infonce_loss"] = infonce_loss(
            pred_emb, tgt_emb, cfg.loss.infonce.temperature
        )
        output["reg_loss"] = output["infonce_loss"]
    else:
        raise ValueError(f"Unsupported loss.regularizer.type: {reg_type}")

    if emb.size(1) > 1:
        output["temporal_hinge_loss"] = temporal_hinge_loss(
            emb[:, :-1],
            emb[:, 1:],
            margin=hinge_cfg.margin,
            metric="cosine",
            squared=hinge_cfg.squared,
        )
    else:
        output["temporal_hinge_loss"] = emb.new_tensor(0.0)

    output["loss"] = (
        output["pred_loss"]
        + reg_lambd * output["reg_loss"]
        + hinge_cfg.weight * output["temporal_hinge_loss"]
    )
    if output["rollout_loss"] is not None:
        rollout_weight = cfg.loss.rollout.get("weight", 0.0)
        output["loss"] = output["loss"] + rollout_weight * output["rollout_loss"]
    # Approximate monitor only: on spherical latents this uses chord-space
    # displacements, not a geodesic straightness measure on the manifold.
    output["temporal_straightness"] = temporal_straightness(emb)

    losses_dict = {
        f"{stage}/{k}": v.detach()
        for k, v in output.items()
        if "loss" in k and torch.is_tensor(v)
    }
    metrics_dict = {
        f"{stage}/temporal_straightness": output["temporal_straightness"].detach()
    }
    self.log_dict({**losses_dict, **metrics_dict}, on_step=True, sync_dist=True)
    return output

    )

            "optimizer": dict(cfg.optimizer),
            "scheduler": {"type": "LinearWarmupCosineAnnealingLR"},
            "interval": "epoch",
        },
    }

    data_module = spt.data.DataModule(train=train, val=val)
    world_model = spt.Module(
        model=world_model,
        forward=partial(swm_forward, cfg=cfg),
        optim=optimizers,
    )

    ##########################

    ##########################

    run_id = cfg.get("subdir") or ""
    run_dir = Path(swm.data.utils.get_cache_dir(), run_id)

    if backend == "swanlab" and cfg.swanlab.enabled:

    run_dir.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
    with open(run_dir / "config.yaml", "w") as f:

    object_dump_callback = ModelObjectCallBack(
        dirpath=run_dir,
        filename=cfg.output_model_name,
        epoch_interval=1,
    )

    trainer = pl.Trainer(
        **cfg.trainer,
        callbacks=[object_dump_callback],
        num_sanity_val_steps=1,
        logger=logger,
        enable_checkpointing=True,
    )

    manager = spt.Manager(
        trainer=trainer,
        module=world_model,
        data=data_module,
        ckpt_path=run_dir / f"{cfg.output_model_name}_weights.ckpt",
    )

    manager()
    return

### Assistant - 2026-04-25 04:35:09 EDT

````
已实现并推送。

提交：
`daf767d Add dynamic temporal margin for SWM`

已推到三个远端的 `ag/dev`：
- `qun-team/le-wm`
- `Holosemantix/le-wm`
- `qun-team/wm_exp`

实现要点：
- 默认关闭，不影响现有 SWM baseline。
- 启用后使用零初始化线性 head：`Linear(2 * embed_dim, 1)`。
- 输入是 `[sg(z_t), sg(act_emb_t)]`，不引入 MLP hidden_dim。
- 使用 mean-normalized score，不需要 `beta`。
- 初始等价于固定 `base_margin`，之后只学习 transition-wise margin 分配。
- 记录诊断指标：`temporal_hinge_active_ratio`、`temporal_margin_mean/std/p10/p50/p90`。

启用示例：
```bash
python train_swm.py \
  loss.temporal_hinge.weight=0.001 \
  loss.temporal_hinge.dynamic.enabled=True \
  loss.temporal_hinge.dynamic.base_margin=0.5
```

验证：
- `python3 -m py_compile train_swm.py`
- `git diff --check -- train_swm.py config/train/swm.yaml`
````
